semiample extension remains an expectation in the source. Uniform closure
uses a fast subsequence with summable errors rather than a decreasing shift
of every prescribed sequence. Smooth maximum regularization and all earlier

universal envelope continuity. The literal Archimedean real-ball step
requires typed complexification; the book uses the non-Archimedean proof.

The first repairs distinguish affine analytification from a compact affinoid
spectrum, state integrality where the valuation cone has one trivial vertex,
retain boundedness/energy conditions for filtration constructions, and replace
an unrestricted comparison promise by the book's proved domains. The reader's
guide now includes Chapters 70--72 and avoids sending complex readers through
Chapters 60--62 twice. Examples and formal theorem/proof environments remain
unchanged.

## Sequential problem queue

Use the exact ordered identifiers in
[the active inventory](open_problem_atomic_inventory_20260824.md): 42 atoms
in twelve formal problem environments, tier totals (0, 3, 7, 15, 17).
Begin with P02.1, then P02.2, P02.3, P02.4, and continue in inventory order.
Work on one problem campaign at a time. A serious unsuccessful attack remains
an open problem; partial progress is recorded with its exact scope and does
not retire the atom or satisfy the full problem's completion criteria.

For each campaign, freeze the task, completion criteria, settings, source
revision, and runtime version. Preserve mathematical notes and failed routes.
Separate exploration, source checking, requirements, reconstruction, and
independent adversarial review. The requested `gpt-6.1-sol`/`xhigh` applies
to new campaign profiles and research calls; historical model names and
settings remain unchanged.

Xean acceptance alone does not promote a new theorem into the manuscript.
Keep candidates outside the active TeX closure until the repository's
[verification protocol](rethlas_verification_protocol.md), primary-source
locator checks, boundary tests, receipt requirements, and integration audit
are satisfied. Never convert a failed, conditional, or unrun attempt into
a solved status.

## Related directions and recent arXiv literature after polishing

Start this phase after Chapters 1--72, Appendices A--N, front matter and
the whole-book coherence checks have passed the renewed reading gates.
Use the revised book's themes and unresolved interfaces to guide the search;
record query dates and the exact arXiv identifiers, versions and revision
dates. Check for later revisions, published versions, corrections and
withdrawals before treating a preprint claim as current.

For each candidate, read the primary statement and proof in context, record
all field, regularity, positivity, boundedness and energy hypotheses, and
verify how its notation and normalizations match the book. Separate proved
results from conditional results, conjectures and proposed research routes.
Check the proof's imported dependencies and boundary examples. Resolve
conflicts with existing text before drafting an integration.

Integrate only material supported by these checks. New chapters must add a
coherent topic with prerequisites, proofs or precisely scoped imports,
examples, references and links to the surrounding chapters. Existing-chapter
updates must reconcile dependent statements and the book's problem inventory.
Run the repository's relevant reference, scope, build and rendered-page
checks after each integration. Preserve candidates and unresolved concerns
outside the active manuscript; do not present an unverified argument as a
theorem or infer that an open problem is solved from a narrower result.

and Appendix L record the comparison scope and attribution. All 2,243
primary-source text lines were read; nine selected actual source PDF pages
were viewed. The general subvariety theorem is not a load-bearing import.

gates. The completed second-reading artifacts remain untouched, and all 42

and [manifest](lin_face_integration_manifest_20261004.json). Wu's broader
claims remain outside the active manuscript pending the recorded source
and dependency checks. Literature research continues in the cloud.

dependencies remain the next research steps. The active book remains the

### Uniform FS composition and full hybrid limit, 4 October 2026

The [FS composition](research_dossiers/li_uniform_fs_composition_20261004.md)
combines the fixed-matrix, Bergman, Fourier and uniform domination steps
under their explicit geometric inputs. It handles inactive chamber rows,
chooses discarded mass before the fixed-degree radius, obtains error
\(C_*/l\), and bounds each finite coefficient vector. The
[hybrid passage](research_dossiers/li_hybrid_limit_passage_20261004.md)
constructs every-small-fibre neighborhood density from SNC coordinates,
uses joint FS continuity and coefficient compactness, writes the MA
comparison on a restricted sequence base, and upgrades the uniquely
identified central limit to full hybrid continuity using subnets and
fixed continuous comparison families. Punctured-total-family continuity
and connected/geometrically integral fibres are explicit; fibrewise
smoothness and scalar-subsequence uniqueness are not substituted for
them. Boucksom arXiv v2 was freshly checked in 876 scoped extracted lines
and thirteen actual pages, including the real-weight FS input and
absolute-value CLN estimates. See the [audit](li_uniform_fs_and_hybrid_limit_audit_20261004.md)
and [manifest](li_uniform_fs_and_hybrid_limit_manifest_20261004.json).
The general geometric module, measure/remainder, Skoda and volume imports
remain application gates. No external verifier, new calculation,
principal general CY theorem or solved problem is claimed. The checked
4,244-page book and all 42 statuses are unchanged. The next research step
is to examine the geometric section-module and local expansion inputs.

### Section module, faithful expansion and analytic remainder, 4 October 2026

The [geometric module reduction](research_dossiers/li_geometric_section_module_20261004.md)
uses a supplied projective analytic/algebraic germ model, perfect derived
cohomology and finite coefficient descent to obtain the full small-fibre
section basis and its completed generic space. It avoids nonexistent
nonzero-parameter evaluation of the whole germ ring. A finite analytic-path
evaluation determinant proves faithful formal Laurent expansion, including
repeated components and vertex charts. The [convergent remainder proof](research_dossiers/li_convergent_chamber_remainder_20261004.md)
uses a compact interior chamber gap and Cauchy bounds to control the entire
infinite Taylor tail, retains zeros of leading coefficient functions, and
checks arbitrary coefficient tuples and frame-weight comparison. Cached
Stacks 0B91/05LY and Bingener's exact retyping locators were checked; 658
fresh scoped Bingener lines and six actual pages were inspected. See the
[audit](li_section_module_and_remainder_audit_20261004.md) and
[manifest](li_section_module_and_remainder_manifest_20261004.json).
The global model and analytic/cohomological imports, CY volume/generic-mass
and uniform Skoda inputs, and the reduced-SNC dyadic scope remain explicit
